Line search update:
```
def multiplicative_update(logp, g, step):
    lp = logp - step*g
    lp = lp - np.max(lp)
    np.clip(lp, -700, 0, out=lp) # no state? use np.maximum.
    p = np.exp(lp)
    s = p.sum()
    return p/s, lp - np.log(s)
```

- `softmax_logp(logp):` returns p normalized and logp normalized? Need top-level.
```
def normalize_logp(logp):
    m = np.max(logp)
    lp = logp - m
    lp = np.clip(lp, LOG_MIN, 0.0) # clip? If we clip, sum p maybe not 1 until normalize.
    p = np.exp(lp)
    s = p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       p = np.full_like(logp, 1.0/logp.size)
       lp = np.log(p)
    else:
       p = p / s
       lp = lp - np.log(s)
    return p, lp
```
 Clipping after subtract max: values <LOG_MIN set to LOG_MIN, which slightly adds mass; normalization corrects. But if many set floor, p has minimum mass. LOG_MIN = -700. Good. However if we clip, `lp` might no longer correspond exactly to ln p due normalization? p=exp(lp_norm that used clipping)/s, but updating logp with clipped may bias. If we want exact, better not clip for normal operations, but prevent underflow. We can use `lp = np.where(lp < LOG_MIN, LOG_MIN, lp)`. The resulting p after normalization has those coords floor/s. It's okay. We'll use it.

But if some coordinates should be zero, floor keeps tiny; no issue.

- `score_p(p):` returns q,maxq,score.
```
def score_p(p):
    q = np.convolve(p, p)
    maxq = float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
    return (2.0 * p.size * maxq), q, maxq
```
But for line search maybe avoid returning q? We need q for beta in main; for candidate just score. Define `score_p_only(p)` and `score_and_q(p)`. Use `np.max(q)`. Ensure p finite.

- `gradient_weights(w,p):` h = sig.correlate(w,p,mode='valid'). But if output length not n due? We'll pad/slice:
```
def weighted_gradient(w,p):
    # w length 2n-1, p length n
    h = scipy.signal.correlate(w, p, mode='valid')
    if h.size != p.size:
       # fallback via manual? use full conv
       c = np.convolve(w, p[::-1])
       start = p.size - 1
       h = c[start:start+p.size]
    return 2.0*h
```
 Fallback top-level. Use `mode='valid'` might be `np.convolve(w, p[::-1], mode='valid')` exactly; maybe simpler use np.convolve to avoid scipy signal? `np.convolve(w, p[::-1], mode='valid')` returns valid length len(w)-len(p)+1 = n.    cur_score = 2*n*maxq
```
   if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12: update best; no_improve=0 else no_improve++
   # occasional beta update
```

def softmax_weights(q, maxq, beta, active_tol):
    if beta > 1e-6:
        scaled = beta*(q - maxq)
        # clamp to avoid underflow? scaled <=0, min -700
        e = np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, -700.0))
        s = e.sum()
        if s > 0: return e/s
    active = q >= maxq - tol_factor*maxq - 1e-15
    w = np.zeros_like(q); w[active]=1.0; return w/(w.sum())

def optimize_from(p_init, best_score, deadline, seed=1):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = p_init.copy(); p = np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s>0: p/=s
    logp, p = normalize_logp(np.log(p))
    q, maxq = score_and_q(p)
    score = 2*p.size*maxq
    if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_p=p.copy()
    step = 20.0
    beta = 2000.0
    no_improve=0
    iteration=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       iteration +=1
       # schedule beta
       if iteration % 100 == 0:
           beta = next beta? we can define as list tuple and index by (iteration//100)%len

       g = gradient_from_weights(w,p)

```
FW function:
```
def fw_safe_search(p, initial_best_score, max_iters=50, deadline=None):
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_only(p)
    p_cur=p.copy()
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        q, maxq = score_and_q(p_cur)
        score = 2*p_cur.size*maxq
        if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_p=p_cur.copy()
        # choose active band average subgradient? Use weights active and g ; then min g linear.
        tol=max(1e-12,1e-7*maxq)
        w = np.zeros_like(q); w[q >= maxq-tol]=1.0; w/=w.sum()
        g = gradient_from_weights(w,p_cur)
        j=int(np.argmin(g))
        # try gammas
        improved=False
        for gamma in (0.005,0.02,0.08,0.25,0.6):
            cand = (1.0-gamma)*p_cur
            cand[j] += gamma
            # clip/normalize
            cand = np.clip(cand,0,None)
            s=cand.sum(); if s>0: cand/=s
            sc = score_only(cand)
            if sc < best_score:
               best_score=sc; best_p=cand.copy()
               if sc < score:
                  p_cur=cand; improved=True; break
        if not improved:
            # maybe try all coordinates with low g? top 5
            cand search? 
            break
    return best_p, best_score
```

Top-level:
```
def smooth_objective(z, beta):
    p, lp = normalize_logp(z)
    q = np.convolve(p,p)
    maxq = q.max()
    val = maxq + (1.0/beta)*np.log(np.sum(np.exp(beta*(q-maxq))))
    return float(val)
```
But val is max approx, not multiplied by2n. Gradient should correspond to same objective (maxq), not score; okay.
```
def smooth_gradient(z, beta):
    p, _ = normalize_logp(z)
    q = np.convolve(p,p)
    maxq = q.max()
    scaled = beta*(q-maxq)
    e = np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, LOG_FLOOR))
    s=e.sum(); w=e/s
    gp = gradient_from_weights(w,p)
    dot = float(np.dot(p,gp))
    gz = gp - p*dot
    return gz
```
Call minimize:
```
from scipy.optimize import minimize
z0 = np.log(p_best)
for beta in [1000, 5000, 20000]:
   if time < deadline-60:
       res = minimize(smooth_objective, z0, args=(beta,), jac=smooth_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': 60, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxls':10})
       if res.success or res.x finite:
          p_candidate,_=normalize_logp(res.x)
          sc=score_only(p_candidate)
          if sc<best: update; z0=log(p_candidate)
```
But objective val maxq; minimizing it equivalent to score; gradient correct? We omit scale 2n; fine. The logsumexp of beta(q-max) plus max: derivative w.r.t p = ∑w_k grad q_k, as used. In z gradient using softmax chain. Good.
```

```
So top-level functions:
```
def softmax_precise(z):
   if z.size==0...
   m=np.max(z)
   e=np.exp(z-m)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: uniform
   return e/s
def normalize_logp(logp): # uses clipping for multiplicative
   m=np.max(logp)
   lp=logp-m
   lp=np.where(lp<LOG_FLOOR, LOG_FLOOR, lp)
   p=np.exp(lp)
   s=p.sum()
   if not finite... uniform
   p/=s
   return p, lp - np.log(s) # but lp not can have min floor.
```
 In `propose`, for L-BFGS x, use softmax_precise to get p.

Objective:
```
def lbfgs_objective(z, beta):
   z = np.asarray(z, float)
   if z.shape[0] ==0 return 1e100
   zc = z - np.mean(z)
   p = softmax_precise(zc)
   q = np.convolve(p,p)
   maxq = q.max()
   scaled = beta*(q-maxq)
   # scaled min could be large negative if beta huge; exp underflow to0
   e=np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, -745.0))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: val=maxq
   else: val=maxq + (1.0/beta)*math.log(s)
   return float(val)
```
Gradient:
```
def lbfgs_gradient(z,beta):
   z=...; zc=z-mean; p=softmax_precise(zc); q=conv; maxq=...
   scaled=...; e=...; w=e/s
   gp=gradient_from_weights(w,p)
   dot=float(np.dot(p,gp))
   return gp - p*dot
```
This gradient sums 0. Good.
```

```
Pseudo:
```
def refine_multiplicative(p_init, best_score, deadline, seed=1):
    n = int(p_init.size)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p = np.array(p_init, float).copy()
    p = np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: uniform; else p/=s
    logp, p = normalize_logp(np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)))
    q, maxq = score_and_q(p)
    score = 2.0*n*maxq
    if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_p=p.copy()
    step = 20.0
    betas = (800, 3000, 12000, 30000, 500, 0.0, 20000)
    beta_idx = 0
    no_improve=0
    iter=0
    best_info = (p.copy(), score, q, maxq)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       iter += 1
       if iter % 100 == 1:
          beta_idx = (beta_idx + 1) % len(betas)
       beta = betas[beta_idx]
       # Current score if p changed? already
       if not np.isfinite(score) or score >= 1e100: compute
       # weights
       if beta > 0:
           scaled = beta*(q - maxq)
           e = np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, -745.0))
           s = e.sum()
           if s <= 0 or not finite: w = zeros; w[q.argmax()]=1
           else: w = e/s
       else:
           tol = max(1e-14, 1e-9*maxq)
           w = (q >= maxq - tol).astype(float); w /= w.sum()
       g = gradient_from_weights(w,p)
       if not finite(g): break
       # candidate line search with current g
       etas = (step*0.05, step*0.2, step, step*5, step*20)
       best_cand=None; best_sc=score; best_qic=None; best_maxic=maxq; best_eta=0.0
       for eta in etas:
          if eta <= 1e-100: continue
          lp = logp - eta*g
          cand, _ = normalize_logp(lp)
          sc, qc, maxqc = score_and_q(cand)
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
             best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand; best_qic=qc; best_maxic=maxqc; best_eta=eta
       # if no candidate, try gamma steps of multiplicative with exact active? Also very small eta
       if best_cand is None:
          # exact active gradient
          tol = max(1e-14, 1e-8*maxq)
          active = q >= maxq - tol
          wa = np.zeros_like(q); wa[active]=1.0; wa /= wa.sum()
          ga = gradient_from_weights(wa,p)
          for eta in (step*0.01, step*0.05, step*0.2, step):
             lp = logp - eta*ga
             cand, _ = normalize_logp(lp)
             sc, qc, maxqc = score_and_q(cand)
             if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc=sc; best_cand=cand; best_qic=qc; best_maxic=maxqc; best_eta=eta
                # switch ga for log update? we'll recompute log from p best
       if best_cand is None:
          no_improve += 1
          step = max(step*0.8, 1e-6)
          # if large no improve, random perturb or FW
          if no_improve > 80:
             # random log normal small, curr p
             amp = 0.05 * min(1.0, no_improve/100.0)
             trial_log = logp + amp*rng.standard_normal(n)
             cand, logp2 = normalize_logp(trial_log)
             sc, qc, maxqc = score_and_q(cand)
             if sc < score:
                p=cand; logp=logp2; q=qc; maxq=maxqc; score=sc
                no_improve=0
             else:
                # try FW condition
                # choose 20 fw iterations quickly
                p2, sc2 = fw_short_refine(p, q, maxq, score, 20, deadline)
                if sc2 < score:
                   p=p2; logp,_=normalize_logp(np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))); q,maxq=score_and_q(p); score=sc2; no_improve=0
                else:
                   # reset step and beta maybe
                   step=20.0
                   # if best_score not improved for long? maybe restart from best_p
                   p=best_p.copy()
                   logp,_ = normalize_logp(np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)))
                   q,maxq=score_and_q(p); score=2*n*maxq
                   no_improve=0
                   step=10.0
          continue
       # apply best candidate
       if best_sc < score:
          no_improve=0
          p=best_cand
          logp, p = normalize_logp(np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)))
          q=best_qic; maxq=best_maxic; score=best_sc
          # adapt step
          if best_eta > step*1.5:
             step=min(step*1.3, 1000.0)
          elif best_eta < step*0.5:
             step=max(step*0.7, 1e-6)
       else:
          # impossible because best_cand only if < score
          pass
       if score < best_score:
          best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); print...
       if iteration % 25 ==0: print
       if best_score < 1.35: break
    return best_p,best_score
```
Need define `fw_short_refine` top-level. Let's implement robust:
```
def fw_short_refine(p, q, maxq, score, max_iters, deadline):
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    cur=p.copy()
    cur_q=q; cur_max=maxq; cur_score=score
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       # if cur_q not current? recompute due p change maybe
       w active
       g = gradient...
       j=argmin(g)
       improved=False
       for gamma in (0.005,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.25):
          cand=(1-gamma)*cur; cand[j]+=gamma
          cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); s=cand.sum(); if s>0 cand/=s
          sc,qc,mqc=score_and_q(cand)
          if sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_p=cand.copy()
             if sc < cur_score:
                cur=cand; cur_q=qc; cur_max=mqc; cur_score=sc; improved=True; break
       if not improved: break
    return best_p,best_score
```
But `p` may have tiny zeros; (1-gamma)*cur keeps all positive if cur positive; good. If cur has zeros? from normalize has floor? maybe. Fine.

This FW uses exact active gradient; if constraints plateau, min g may be coordinate not in active? It may move mass to a coordinate that reduces many active constraints.

Maybe too aggressive; line search gamma small.

But in `refine`, when no improve and no_improve>80, random reset to best_p could undo progress in p but keep best. That's okay if stuck.

Then propose can do final random local search.

Final random local search top-level:
```
def random_local_search(p_init, best_score, deadline, seed):
    p=copy; best=p; score=best_score? compute
    rng; for i in range(2000):
       amp = 0.05 + 0.05*(1 - i/2000)?
       logp=np.log(p)
       # Maybe perturb a random block to avoid destroying structure:
       idx = rng.integers...
       block = rng.uniform...
       p_trial = p.copy(); p_trial[block] *= exp(amp*randn(block))
       normalize; score; if < best update
    return best, score
```
This uses exact score each trial O(n^2). 500 trials maybe 10s. Use if time > 100s. It may not improve convex optimum much but okay.
```

```
Candidate generation details:
```
def make_power(n, alpha, shift):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    base = i + shift
    if np.any(base <= 0): base=np.maximum(base, 1e-6)
    p = base**(-alpha)
    p = np.clip(p,0,None)
    s=p.sum(); if s>0: p/=s; else uniform
    return p

def score_and_q(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    q = np.convolve(p, p)
    if q.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        return (np.inf, q, 0.0)
    maxq = float(np.max(q))
    score = 2.0 * float(p.size) * maxq
    return score, q, maxq
```

Let's implement `clean_p(p)` top-level:
```
def clean_p(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    p[p<0]=0.0
    p[np.isinf(p)|np.isnan(p)]=0.0
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(p.size, 1.0/p.size)
    return p/s
```
 No allocation big? okay.
```

def score_and_q(p):

    maxq = float(np.max(q))

def gradient_from_weights(w,p):
    h = np.convolve(w, p[::-1], mode='valid')
    n=p.size
    if h.size < n:
       # pad/trunc?
       c=np.convolve(w,p[::-1])
       h=c[n-1:n-1+n]
    elif h.size > n:
       h=h[:n]
    return 2.0*h.astype(np.float64)
```

`softmax_precise` top-level:
```
def softmax_precise(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size ==0: return empty
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return full uniform
    m=np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(z-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return uniform
    return e/s
```
 Good.
```

def random_local_search(p_init, best_score, deadline, seed):
    p = clean_p(p_init)
    q,maxq = score_and_q(p); score = 2n maxq
    best_score = min(best_score, score); best_p=p.copy() if score<best...
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n=p.size
    for it in range(3000):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       # choose block width and multiplicative noise
       width = int(rng.integers(max(1,n//200), max(2,n//8)))
       start = int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
       amp = 0.1 + 0.2*rng.random()
       noise = rng.standard_normal(width) * amp
       trial = p.copy()
       trial[start:start+width] *= np.exp(noise)
       # occasional global small noise
       if rng.random()<0.2:
           trial *= np.exp(0.02*rng.standard_normal(n))
       sc,_,_ = score_and_q(trial)
       if sc < best_score:
           best_score=sc; best_p=trial/clean? score normalized; p = clean_p(trial)
           if it%100==0: print
    return best_p,best_score

'''Search nonnegative sequences whose self-convolution is flat. Starts from inverse-square-root endpoint densities, optimizes a smoothed max-convex objective with L-BFGS-B on softmax weights, then refines by exact multiplicative line search, Frank-Wolove, and block random local search.'''

```
One more important thing: The objective in L-BFGS: `lbfgs_objective(z,beta)` returns val = maxq + (1/beta)*log(sum exp(beta(q-maxq))). But gradient of this val is w? Let's verify: derivative of 1/beta log∑ e^{β(q-maxq)}: w_k (∂q_k - ∂maxq). But w sums1, ∑w ∂maxq_k? maxq is max over Q; for smoothed with subtract maxq, derivative of maxq term? We approximated gradient using w only, ignoring derivative of maxq subtraction. Since maxq is function of p, f = M + 1/β log∑ e^{β(q-M)} = 1/β log∑ e^{β q}. Because M cancels? Actually log sum e^{β(q-M)} + M = log∑ e^{β q}/β. So f = (1/β) log∑ e^{β q}. Derivative = ∑ w_k ∂q_k, where w_k = e^{βq_k}/∑e^{βq_j} (same as using shifted). No ∂M term. Correct. So gradient w is softmax of q, independent of max. Good. Our w using q-max is same. Good.

L-BFGS objective val is f, not approximate max plus smooth, but equal. Good. For large beta, f≈maxq + small. The gradient is w. Great.
```

```python
"""
Search nonnegative height sequences whose full self-convolution is as flat as
possible.  The objective is scale invariant:

    Q = 2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2)

The code starts from inverse-square-root one-sided endpoint densities, which
already give low convolution peaks, and then optimizes a smoothed max-convex
objective in the softmax parameterization with L-BFGS-B.  After that it
refines using exact multiplicative line search, a short Frank-Wolfe fallback,
and a conservative block random local search.  The evaluator is intentionally
only used for optional validation; the internal score is the same normalized
convolution peak.
"""

import time
import math

import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT = True
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT = False

LOG_FLOOR = -745.0


def clean_p(p):
    """Return a nonnegative finite float array normalized to sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    p[~np.isfinite(p)] = 0.0
    p[p < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(p.size, 1.0 / float(p.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def softmax_precise(z):
    """Stable softmax without artificial floors, for bounded L-BFGS variables."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.ndim != 1 or z.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / float(z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def normalize_logp(logp):
    """
    Convert log-probabilities to a normalized probability vector.

    The floor prevents underflow; since the target is only numerical, tiny
    floor mass is harmless.
    """
    logp = np.asarray(logp, dtype=np.float64)
    if logp.ndim != 1 or logp.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    logp = np.where(np.isfinite(logp), logp, LOG_FLOOR)
    m = float(np.max(logp))
    lp = logp - m
    lp = np.maximum(lp, LOG_FLOOR)
    p = np.exp(lp)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(logp.size, 1.0 / float(logp.size), dtype=np.float64)
        lp = np.log(p)
    else:
        p = p / s
        lp = lp - math.log(s)
    return p, lp


def score_and_q(p):
    """
    Internal exact score for a normalized nonnegative sequence.

    Returns (score, q, max_q) where q = convolve(clean_p(p), clean_p(p)).
    """
    p = clean_p(p)
    if p.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=np.float64), float("inf")
    q = np.convolve(p, p)
    if q.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), q, float("inf")
    max_q = float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(max_q):
        return float("inf"), q, max_q
    score = 2.0 * float(p.size) * max_q
    return score, q, max_q


def score_only(p):
    """Return just the score."""
    return score_and_q(p)[0]


def gradient_from_weights(w, p):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k * q_k where q = p * p.

    For q_k = sum_{i+j=k} p_i p_j,
        d q_k / d p_l = 2 p_{k-l} if 0 <= k-l < n else 0.
    Therefore
        g_l = 2 sum_{r=0}^{n-1} w_{l+r} p_r.
    This is the valid part of convolve(w, reverse(p)).
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        h = np.convolve(w, p[::-1], mode="valid")
    except Exception:
        h = None
    if h is None or h.size != n:
        full = np.convolve(w, p[::-1])
        start = n - 1
        h = full[start:start + n]
        if h.size < n:
            tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            tmp[: h.size] = h
            h = tmp
        elif h.size > n:
            h = h[:n]
    g = 2.0 * np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
    return g


def make_power_sequence(n, alpha, shift=0.5):
    """A one-sided endpoint-density candidate: p_i ~ (i + shift)^(-alpha)."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    base = i + float(shift)
    base[base <= 0.0] = 1e-6
    p = np.power(base, -float(alpha))
    p = clean_p(p)
    return p


def make_initial_candidates(n, seed=12345):
    """Evaluate a handful of analytic starting points and keep the best."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    specs = [
        (0.50, 0.50),
        (0.50, 1.00),
        (0.49, 0.50),
        (0.48, 0.50),
        (0.47, 0.50),
        (0.45, 0.50),
        (0.52, 0.50),
        (0.50, 0.25),
        (0.50, 1.20),
    ]

    for alpha, shift in specs:
        p = make_power_sequence(n, alpha, shift)
        sc = score_only(p)
        if sc < best_score and math.isfinite(sc):
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p

    # Add a few smooth noisy variants so that the optimizer has alternatives.
    base = make_power_sequence(n, 0.50, 0.50)
    for k in range(6):
        amp = 0.03 + 0.12 * float(k) / 5.0
        noise = amp * rng.standard_normal(n)
        p = base * np.exp(noise)
        if k % 3 == 0:
            p = p * (0.8 + 0.4 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(n) / float(n)))
        p = clean_p(p)
        sc = score_only(p)
        if sc < best_score and math.isfinite(sc):
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = clean_p(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        best_score = score_only(best_p)

    return best_p, best_score


def p_to_z(p, lower=-30.0, upper=30.0):
    """Map a probability vector to bounded centered log-weights."""
    p = clean_p(p)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-30))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z = np.clip(z, lower, upper)
    return z


def lbfgs_objective(z, beta):
    """Smooth log-sum-exp proxy for max(q), where q = convolve(p, p)."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.ndim != 1 or z.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    p = softmax_precise(z - float(np.mean(z)))
    q = np.convolve(p, p)
    if q.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    maxq = float(np.max(q))
    if not math.isfinite(maxq):
        return 1e100
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return maxq
    scaled = beta * (q - maxq)
    e = np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, LOG_FLOOR))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return maxq
    val = maxq + (1.0 / beta) * math.log(s)
    return float(val) if math.isfinite(val) else 1e100


def lbfgs_gradient(z, beta):
    """Gradient of lbfgs_objective with respect to the original z."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.ndim != 1 or z.size == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
    p = softmax_precise(z - float(np.mean(z)))
    q = np.convolve(p, p)
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
    maxq = float(np.max(q))
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        # Use an exact subgradient over the active constraints.
        tol = max(1e-14, 1e-8 * maxq)
        w = (q >= maxq - tol).astype(np.float64)
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros_like(q)
            int(np.argmax(q))
            w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
            s = 1.0
        w = w / s
    else:
        scaled = beta * (q - maxq)
        e = np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, LOG_FLOOR))
        s = float(e.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros_like(q)
            w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = e / s
    gp = gradient_from_weights(w, p)
    dot = float(np.dot(p, gp))
    gz = gp - p * dot
    gz = np.where(np.isfinite(gz), gz, 0.0)
    return gz


def run_lbfgs_phase(p_init, best_score, deadline, seed=7):
    """L-BFGS-B refinement on a smoothed proxy."""
    if not HAVE_SCIPY_OPT or minimize is None:
        return p_init, best_score

    p = clean_p(p_init)
    sc = score_only(p)
    if sc < best_score and math.isfinite(sc):
        best_score = sc
        p_best = p.copy()
    else:
        p_best = p.copy()

    z = p_to_z(p_best)
    n = p_best.size
    bounds = [(-30.0, 30.0)] * n

    # A few rounds with increasing emphasis on the active max constraint.
    for beta in (3000.0, 10000.0, 30000.0):
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 90.0:
            break
        if remaining > 700.0:
            maxiter = 90
        elif remaining > 350.0:
            maxiter = 60
        else:
            maxiter = 35
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lbfgs_objective,
                z,
                args=(beta,),
                jac=lbfgs_gradient,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "ftol": 1e-13,
                    "gtol": 1e-5,
                    "maxcor": 30,
                    "maxls": 12,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print("L-BFGS-B error:", repr(exc))
            break

        if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x"):
            continue
        zx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(zx)):
            continue
        cand = softmax_precise(zx - float(np.mean(zx)))
        cand = clean_p(cand)
        csc = score_only(cand)
        if math.isfinite(csc) and csc < best_score:
            print(
                "LBFGS beta={:.1f} improves score to {:.9f} from {:.9f}".format(
                    beta, csc, best_score
                )
            )
            best_score = csc
            p_best = cand.copy()
            z = p_to_z(p_best)
        else:
            # It sometimes helps to keep the smoothed point even if the exact
            # score is flat; try a gentle blend.
            blend = 0.5 * cand + 0.5 * p_best
            blend = clean_p(blend)
            bsc = score_only(blend)
            if math.isfinite(bsc) and bsc < best_score:
                print(
                    "LBFGS blend beta={:.1f} improves score to {:.9f} from {:.9f}".format(
                        beta, bsc, best_score
                    )
                )
                best_score = bsc
                p_best = blend.copy()
                z = p_to_z(p_best)

    return p_best, best_score


def fw_short_refine(p, q, maxq, score, max_iters=25, deadline=None):
    """
    A very short Frank-Wolfe fallback.  It averages exact active constraints,
    takes a linear minimum step, and only moves if an exact line-search point
    improves the score.
    """
    p = clean_p(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = float(score)

    cur = p.copy()
    cur_q = q.copy()
    cur_max = float(maxq)
    cur_score = float(score)

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        if not math.isfinite(cur_score):
            break

        tol = max(1e-14, 1e-7 * cur_max)
        active = cur_q >= cur_max - tol
        active_cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(active))
        if active_cnt <= 0:
            active = np.zeros(cur_q.shape, dtype=bool)
            active[int(np.argmax(cur_q))] = True
        w = active.astype(np.float64)
        w /= float(w.sum())
        g = gradient_from_weights(w, cur)
        if not np.any(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        j = int(np.argmin(g))

 improved = False
        for gamma in (0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.25):
            cand = (1.0 - gamma) * cur
            cand[j] += gamma
            cand = clean_p(cand)
            csc, cq, cmax = score_and_q(cand)
            if math.isfinite(csc) and csc < best_score:
                best_score = csc
                best_p = cand.copy()
                if csc < cur_score:
                    cur = cand
                    cur_q = cq
                    cur_max = cmax
                    cur_score = csc
                    improved = True
                    break

        if not improved:
            break

    return best_p, best_score


def refine_multiplicative(p_init, best_score, deadline, seed=99):
    """
    Exact multiplicative line-search refinement.

    It minimizes a softmax-smoothed convex proxy in probability space:
        p <- p * exp(-eta * grad) / sum(...)
    then chooses eta by exact convolution score.  If the smoothed direction
    stalls, it tries exact active-constraint subgradients, a short Frank-Wolfe
    phase, and finally returns to the best point.
    """
    n = int(np.asarray(p_init, dtype=np.float64).size)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    p = clean_p(p_init)
    score, q, maxq = score_and_q(p)
    if not math.isfinite(score):
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        score, q, maxq = score_and_q(p)

    if score < best_score and math.isfinite(score):
        best_score = score
        best_p = p.copy()
    else:
        best_p = p.copy()

    logp, p = normalize_logp(np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)))

    step = 25.0
    betas = (1000.0, 4000.0, 16000.0, 40000.0, 500.0, 0.0, 20000.0)
    beta_idx = 0
    no_improve = 0
    stagnant_best = 0
    iteration = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        iteration += 1
        if iteration % 120 == 1:
            beta_idx = (beta_idx + 1) % len(betas)
        beta = float(betas[beta_idx])

        if not math.isfinite(score):
            score, q, maxq = score_and_q(p)

        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            stagnant_best = 0
        else:
            stagnant_best += 1
        if stagnant_best > 1200:
            break

        # Build weights over convolution constraints.
        if beta > 0.0:
            scaled = beta * (q - maxq)
            e = np.exp(np.maximum(scaled, LOG_FLOOR))
            s = float(e.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                w = np.zeros(q.shape, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
            else:
                w = e / s
        else:
            tol = max(1e-14, 1e-9 * maxq)
            w = (q >= maxq - tol).astype(np.float64)
            s = float(w.sum())
            if s <= 0.0:
                w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
            else:
                w /= s

        g = gradient_from_weights(w, p)
        if not np.any(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        tried_g = (g,)
        etas_base = (0.05, 0.20, 1.00, 4.00, 16.00)

        best_trial_p = None
        best_trial_score = score
        best_trial_q = q
        best_trial_maxq = maxq
        best_trial_eta = 0.0

        # Try the smoothed direction.
        for factor in etas_base:
            eta = step * factor
            if eta <= 1e-12:
                continue
            lp = logp - eta * g
            cand, _ = normalize_logp(lp)
            csc, cq, cmax = score_and_q(cand)
            if math.isfinite(csc) and csc < best_trial_score - 1e-14:
                best_trial_score = csc
                best_trial_p = cand
                best_trial_q = cq
                best_trial_maxq = cmax
                best_trial_eta = eta

        # If the smoothed direction did not help, also try an active average.
        if best_trial_p is None:
            tol = max(1e-14, 1e-8 * maxq)
            w_act = (q >= maxq - tol).astype(np.float64)
            s_act = float(w_act.sum())
            if s_act <= 0:
                w_act[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
            else:
                w_act /= s_act
            g_act = gradient_from_weights(w_act, p)
            tried_g = (g_act,)
            for factor in (0.01, 0.05, 0.20, 1.00, 4.00):
                eta = step * factor
                if eta <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                lp = logp - eta * g_act
                cand, _ = normalize_logp(lp)
                csc, cq, cmax = score_and_q(cand)
                if math.isfinite(csc) and csc < best_trial_score - 1e-14:
                    best_trial_score = csc
                    best_trial_p = cand
                    best_trial_q = cq
                    best_trial_maxq = cmax
                    best_trial_eta = eta

        if best_trial_p is not None:
            p = best_trial_p
            logp, p = normalize_logp(np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)))
            q = best_trial_q
            maxq = best_trial_maxq
            score = best_trial_score
            no_improve = 0
            if best_trial_eta > step * 1.5:
                step = min(step * 1.25, 5000.0)
            elif best_trial_eta < step * 0.5:
                step = max(step * 0.75, 1e-6)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            step = max(step * 0.80, 1e-6)

            if no_improve > 100:
                # First try a short FW phase from the current point.
                p_fw, score_fw = fw_short_refine(
                    p, q, maxq, score, max_iters=20, deadline=deadline
                )
                if math.isfinite(score_fw) and score_fw < best_score:
                    best_score = score_fw
                    best_p = p_fw.copy()
                if math.isfinite(score_fw) and score_fw < score:
                    p = p_fw
                    logp, p = normalize_logp(np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)))
                    q, maxq = score_and_q(p)
                    score = float(score_and_q(p)[0])
                    no_improve = 0
                    stagnant_best = 0
                    continue

                # Then a small random perturbation, accepted only if exact better.
                amp = 0.03 + 0.08 * float(rng.random())
                trial_log = logp + amp * rng.standard_normal(n)
                cand, cand_logp = normalize_logp(trial_log)
                csc, cq, cmax = score_and_q(cand)
                if math.isfinite(csc) and csc < score:
                    p = cand
                    logp = cand_logp
                    q = cq
                    maxq = cmax
                    score = csc
                    if csc < best_score:
                        best_score = csc
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        stagnant_best = 0
                    no_improve = 0
                    continue

                # Finally, if progress is completely stuck, go back to the best.
                p = best_p.copy()
                logp, p = normalize_logp(np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)))
                q, maxq = score_and_q(p)
                score = float(score_and_q(p)[0])
                no_improve = 0
                step = 25.0

        if iteration % 50 == 1:
            print(
                "mult iter {:6d} score={:.9f} best={:.9f} step={:.3g} beta={:.3g}".format(
                    iteration, score, best_score, step, beta
                )
            )

        if best_score < 1.35:
            break

    return best_p, best_score


def random_local_search(p_init, best_score, deadline, seed=2023):
    """
    Conservative final local search: multiply random blocks by smooth noise,
    accepting only exact improvements.
    """
    p = clean_p(p_init)
    score = score_only(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = min(float(best_score), float(score))
    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 1e100

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return p, best_sc

    lo = max(1, n // 2000)
    hi = max(lo + 1, n // 10)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
        amp = 0.04 + 0.20 * float(rng.random())
        noise = amp * rng.standard_normal(width)

        trial = p.copy()
        trial[start:start + width] *= np.exp(noise)
        if rng.random() < 0.25:
            trial *= np.exp(0.008 * rng.standard_normal(n))

        trial = clean_p(trial)
        sc = score_only(trial)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = trial.copy()
            p = trial.copy()

    return best_p, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Generate the best nonnegative coefficient sequence found before timeout.
    """
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1800.0
    deadline = start_time + budget_s - 2.0

    # A length around a few thousand is fast for direct convolution and gives
    # enough degrees of freedom.  The previous construction had length 3809.
    n = 3809

    p, score = make_initial_candidates(n, seed=seed + 1)
    print("initial best score = {:.9f}".format(score))

    # L-BFGS phase if enough time remains.
    if HAVE_SCIPY_OPT and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 160.0:
        p, score = run_lbfgs_phase(p, score, deadline, seed=seed + 11)
        print("after L-BFGS best score = {:.9f}".format(score))

    # Exact multiplicative refinement.  Reserve a little time for the final
    # randomized local search.
    remaining_after_lbfgs = deadline - time.monotonic()
    reserve = 60.0 if remaining_after_lbfgs > 350.0 else (15.0 if remaining_after_lbfgs > 80.0 else 0.0)
    refine_deadline = deadline - reserve
    if time.monotonic() < refine_deadline:
        p, score = refine_multiplicative(p, score, refine_deadline, seed=seed + 101)
        print("after multiplicative best score = {:.9f}".format(score))

    # Conservative block random local search.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p, score = random_local_search(p, score, deadline, seed=seed + 102)
        print("after random local best score = {:.9f}".format(score))

    # Final safety normalization, all values nonnegative and below the cap.
    p = clean_p(p)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    p = clean_p(p)

    out = [float(x) for x in p]

    # Optional validation.  Even if this failed for some reason, the internal
    # sequence is still returned.
    try:
        ev = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        print("validate evaluate_sequence = {:.9f}".format(ev))
    except Exception as exc:
        print("validate exception:", repr(exc))

    print("final length = {}, internal score = {:.9f}".format(len(out), score))
    return out
```
